[CTX:L62]      
[OLD:L62]    - ## [5.11.2](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/releases/tag/5.11.2]
[NEW:L63]    + ## [5.12.0](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/releases/tag/5.12.0)
[NEW:L64]    + 
[NEW:L65]    + Released on 2026-05-04. All issues associated with this milestone can be found using this [filter](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/milestone/97?closed=1).
[NEW:L66]    + 
[NEW:L67]    + ### Added
[NEW:L68]    + - FreeBSD compilation support.
[NEW:L69]    +   - Added by [Al Hoang](https://github.com/hoanga) in Pull Request [#4029](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4029).
[NEW:L70]    + 
[NEW:L71]    + ### Updated
[NEW:L72]    + - Various CI configurations.
[NEW:L73]    +   - Updated by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L74]    + 
[NEW:L75]    + ### Fixed
[NEW:L76]    + - Rare race in `Request.suspend()` and `Request.cancel()` where the state update would be lost if it occurred before the task was created.
[NEW:L77]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L78]    + - `Request.cancel()` incorrect called `Request.finish()` when the underlying task was already `.completed`.
[NEW:L79]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L80]    + - Rare race in `Request.resume()` that could lead to multiple `URLSessionTask`s created.
[NEW:L81]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L82]    + - Repeated calls to `Request.suspend()` and `Request.resume()` could lead to multiple pipeline executions.
[NEW:L83]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L84]    + - Thread-safety issue in `Session.deinit` when accessing internal state.
[NEW:L85]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L86]    + - Rare `Request.finish()` race in `Session.deinit`.
[NEW:L87]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L88]    + - `Session.deinit` called `Request.finish()` on `Request`s that were already finished.
[NEW:L89]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L90]    + - `Request.onHTTPRequest` didn't call the full `Request.cancel()` when the `.cancel` disposition was returned.
[NEW:L91]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L92]    + - Multiple unlikely force unwrap or `unowned self` crashes.
[NEW:L93]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L94]    + - Write to `DataStreamRequest`'s `outputStream` after it was closed.
[NEW:L95]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L96]    + - MIME type parsing, where a single element (`text`) or empty strings would be accepted.
[NEW:L97]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L98]    + - Cancelled `DownloadRequest`s could still trigger retry.
[NEW:L99]    +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L100]   + - `DataRequest.DataTask` and `DownloadRequest.DownloadTask` could miss early cancellation events if they happened before the underlying `Task` was created.
[NEW:L101]   +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L102]   + - 🔥 Multiple issues in `AuthenticationInterceptor`. These fixes slightly change the interceptor's behavior.
[NEW:L103]   +     1. `adapt()` enqueued adaptations, leading to requests restarted with a new credential to not execute the whole adapt pipeline again. `AuthenticationInterceptor` will now let those requests fail with the old credential and retry their whole pipeline with the new credential.
[NEW:L104]   +     2. Concurrent adaptations for the same stale credential could trigger multiple refreshes.
[NEW:L105]   +     3. `retry()` now lazily checks for new credentials rather than capturing eagerly.
[NEW:L106]   +   - Fixed by [Jon Shier](https://github.com/jshier) in Pull Request [#4030](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/pull/4030).
[NEW:L107]   + 
[NEW:L108]   + ---

[CTX:L15]      ## Asking Questions
[CTX:L16]      
[OLD:L18]    - **We don't use GitHub as a support forum.**
[OLD:L19]    - For any usage questions that are not specific to the project itself, please ask on [Stack Overflow](https://stackoverflow.com) instead. By doing so, you'll be more likely to quickly solve your problem, and you'll allow anyone else with the same question to find the answer. This also allows maintainers to focus on improving the project for others.
[OLD:L20]    - 
[OLD:L21]    - If you'd like to discuss Alamofire best practices, common usage patterns, ideas for new features, or ongoing development, please use our [Swift Forums](https://forums.swift.org/c/related-projects/alamofire/).
[NEW:L17]    + For any usage questions that are not specific to Alamofire itself (not just HTTP networking in general), discussion of Alamofire best practices, ideas for new features, or ongoing development, please open a [GitHub Discussion](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/discussions).
[CTX:L18]      
[CTX:L19]      ## Reporting Security Issues
[CTX:L20]      
[CTX:L21]      The Alamofire Software Foundation takes security seriously.
[NEW:L22]    + 
[CTX:L23]      If you discover a security issue, please bring it to our attention right away!
[CTX:L24]      
[OLD:L28]    - Please **DO NOT** file a public issue, instead send your report privately to <security@alamofire.org>. This will help ensure that any vulnerabilities that _are_ found can be [disclosed responsibly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Responsible_disclosure) to any affected parties.
[NEW:L25]    + Please **DO NOT** file a public issue! Instead, send your report privately to <security@alamofire.org>. This will help ensure that any vulnerabilities that _are_ found can be [disclosed responsibly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Responsible_disclosure) to any affected parties.
[CTX:L26]      
[CTX:L27]      ## Reporting Other Issues
[CTX:L28]      
[OLD:L32]    - A great way to contribute to the project is to send a detailed issue when you encounter a problem. We always appreciate a well-written, thorough bug report.
[NEW:L29]    + A great way to contribute to the project is to send a detailed issue when you encounter a problem. We always appreciate a well-written, thorough bug report on [GitHub](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/issues).
[CTX:L30]      
[CTX:L31]      Check that the project issues database doesn't already include that problem or suggestion before submitting an issue. If you find a match, feel free to vote for the issue by adding a reaction. Doing this helps prioritize the most common problems and requests.
[CTX:L32]      
[CTX:L33]      When reporting issues, please fill out our issue template. The information the template asks for will help us review and fix your issue faster.
[CTX:L34]      
[OLD:L38]    - ## Triage Issues [![Open Source Helpers](https://www.codetriage.com/alamofire/alamofire/badges/users.svg)](https://www.codetriage.com/alamofire/alamofire)
[NEW:L35]    + ## Submitting Pull Requests
[CTX:L36]      
[OLD:L40]    - You can triage issues which may include reproducing bug reports or asking for vital information, such as version numbers or reproduction instructions. If you would like to start triaging issues, one easy way to get started is to [subscribe to alamofire on CodeTriage](https://www.codetriage.com/alamofire/alamofire).
[NEW:L37]    + You can contribute by fixing bugs or adding new features. Larger changes MUST be discussed on our [GitHub Discussions](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/discussions) before PR submission, or they may be summarily closed. When submitting a pull request, please add relevant tests and ensure your changes don't break any existing tests (see [Automated Tests](#automated-tests) below).
[CTX:L38]      
[OLD:L42]    - ## Submitting Pull Requests
[NEW:L39]    + ### AI Usage
[NEW:L40]    + 
[NEW:L41]    + All pull requests must disclose the degree to which they were AI tool assisted and PR descriptions should be human written. AI tools may be used to assist in translation, but care should be taken to ensure the AI output remains accurate to the original text and isn't a restyling of it in the AI's voice, lest it be confused with direct AI output.
[CTX:L42]      
[OLD:L44]    - You can contribute by fixing bugs or adding new features. For larger code changes, we recommend first discussing your ideas on our [GitHub Discussions](https://github.com/Alamofire/Alamofire/discussions) or [Swift Forums](https://forums.swift.org/c/related-projects/alamofire/). When submitting a pull request, please add relevant tests and ensure your changes don't break any existing tests (see [Automated Tests](#automated-tests) below).
[NEW:L43]    + Failure to disclose AI usage may lead to summary closing of issues and PRs.
[CTX:L44]      
[CTX:L45]      ### Automated Tests
[CTX:L46]      
[CTX:L49]      In your terminal, run the following commands:
[CTX:L50]      - To install Firewalk: `brew install alamofire/alamofire/firewalk`
[CTX:L51]      - To run and detach the server: `firewalk &`
[OLD:L53]    - - To stop the server, run `kill` and provide the pid output after launch.
[NEW:L52]    + - To stop the server, run `killall firewalk` .
[CTX:L53]

[NEW:L1074]  +     @MainActor
[NEW:L1075]  +     func testThatInterceptorAdaptsNewRequestWithoutDeferringWhileRetryPathRefreshIsInProgress() {
[NEW:L1076]  +         // Given
[NEW:L1077]  +         // When a refresh is triggered by the retry path, adaptOperations is empty. Any new adapt call must NOT be
[NEW:L1078]  +         // deferred, it should proceed immediately with the current credential.
[NEW:L1079]  +         // This is the documented invariant: defer only when adaptOperations is non-empty (i.e., a proactive
[NEW:L1080]  +         // requiresRefresh-driven refresh is running).
[NEW:L1081]  +         let credential = TestCredential()
[NEW:L1082]  +         let authenticator = TestAuthenticator()
[NEW:L1083]  +         let interceptor = AuthenticationInterceptor(authenticator: authenticator, credential: credential)
[NEW:L1084]  +         let session = stored(Session())
[NEW:L1085]  + 
[NEW:L1086]  +         let expect = expectation(description: "both requests should complete")
[NEW:L1087]  +         expect.expectedFulfillmentCount = 2
[NEW:L1088]  + 
[NEW:L1089]  +         var firstResponse: AFDataResponse<Data?>?
[NEW:L1090]  +         var secondResponse: AFDataResponse<Data?>?
[NEW:L1091]  + 
[NEW:L1092]  +         // When
[NEW:L1093]  +         // Request A: gets 401, triggering an async retry-path refresh.
[NEW:L1094]  +         let pathAdapter = PathAdapter(paths: ["/status/401", "/status/200"])
[NEW:L1095]  +         let compositeInterceptor = Interceptor(adapters: [pathAdapter, interceptor], retriers: [interceptor])
[NEW:L1096]  + 
[NEW:L1097]  +         let requestA = session.request(.default, interceptor: compositeInterceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1098]  +             firstResponse = $0
[NEW:L1099]  +             expect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1100]  +         }
[NEW:L1101]  + 
[NEW:L1102]  +         // Request B: adapts while A's refresh may be running. Since adaptOperations is empty,
[NEW:L1103]  +         // B must not be deferred regardless of the current refreshState.
[NEW:L1104]  +         let requestB = session.request(.status(200), interceptor: interceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1105]  +             secondResponse = $0
[NEW:L1106]  +             expect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1107]  +         }
[NEW:L1108]  + 
[NEW:L1109]  +         waitForExpectations(timeout: timeout)
[NEW:L1110]  + 
[NEW:L1111]  +         // Then: both succeed, only one refresh occurred, and B was applied (not deferred).
[NEW:L1112]  +         XCTAssertEqual(firstResponse?.result.isSuccess, true)
[NEW:L1113]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.isSuccess, true)
[NEW:L1114]  +         XCTAssertNotNil(secondResponse?.request?.headers["Authorization"])
[NEW:L1115]  + 
[NEW:L1116]  +         XCTAssertEqual(requestA.retryCount, 1)
[NEW:L1117]  +         XCTAssertEqual(requestB.retryCount, 0)
[NEW:L1118]  + 
[NEW:L1119]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.applyCount, 3)
[NEW:L1120]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.refreshCount, 1)
[NEW:L1121]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.didRequestFailDueToAuthErrorCount, 1)
[NEW:L1122]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.isRequestAuthenticatedWithCredentialCount, 1)
[NEW:L1123]  +     }

[NEW:L1126]  +     func testThatInterceptorAllowsNewRefreshAfterExternalCredentialUpdateFollowingRetryPathFailure() {
[NEW:L1127]  +         // Given
[NEW:L1128]  +         // After a retry-path refresh fails the interceptor enters .failed state, which blocks subsequent retries from
[NEW:L1129]  +         // triggering another refresh. Setting interceptor.credential externally calls updateCredential(_:), which
[NEW:L1130]  +         // increments credentialVersion and resets refreshState to .idle, restoring the ability to refresh.
[NEW:L1131]  +         let credential = TestCredential()
[NEW:L1132]  +         let authenticator = TestAuthenticator(refreshResult: .failure(TestAuthError.refreshNetworkFailure))
[NEW:L1133]  +         let interceptor = AuthenticationInterceptor(authenticator: authenticator, credential: credential)
[NEW:L1134]  +         let session = stored(Session())
[NEW:L1135]  + 
[NEW:L1136]  +         // First, trigger a retry-path refresh that fails, putting interceptor into .failed state.
[NEW:L1137]  +         let firstExpect = expectation(description: "first request should complete")
[NEW:L1138]  +         var firstResponse: AFDataResponse<Data?>?
[NEW:L1139]  + 
[NEW:L1140]  +         session.request(.status(401), interceptor: interceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1141]  +             firstResponse = $0
[NEW:L1142]  +             firstExpect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1143]  +         }
[NEW:L1144]  + 
[NEW:L1145]  +         waitForExpectations(timeout: timeout)
[NEW:L1146]  + 
[NEW:L1147]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.refreshCount, 1)
[NEW:L1148]  +         XCTAssertEqual(firstResponse?.result.isFailure, true)
[NEW:L1149]  + 
[NEW:L1150]  +         // When: credential is externally replaced. updateCredential(_:) resets refreshState to .idle.
[NEW:L1151]  +         interceptor.credential = TestCredential(accessToken: "ext")
[NEW:L1152]  + 
[NEW:L1153]  +         // Second, a new 401 should now trigger a fresh refresh rather than immediately failing with the error cached in
[NEW:L1154]  +         // .failed state.
[NEW:L1155]  +         let secondExpect = expectation(description: "second request should complete")
[NEW:L1156]  +         var secondResponse: AFDataResponse<Data?>?
[NEW:L1157]  + 
[NEW:L1158]  +         session.request(.status(401), interceptor: interceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1159]  +             secondResponse = $0
[NEW:L1160]  +             secondExpect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1161]  +         }
[NEW:L1162]  + 
[NEW:L1163]  +         waitForExpectations(timeout: timeout)
[NEW:L1164]  + 
[NEW:L1165]  +         // Then: a second refresh was attempted, proving .failed was cleared by updateCredential(_:).
[NEW:L1166]  +         // The refresh fails again (same authenticator), but crucially refreshCount is 2, not 1.
[NEW:L1167]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.request?.headers["Authorization"], "ext")
[NEW:L1168]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.isFailure, true)
[NEW:L1169]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.failure?.asAFError?.isRequestRetryError, true)
[NEW:L1170]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.failure?.asAFError?.underlyingError as? TestAuthError, .refreshNetworkFailure)
[NEW:L1171]  + 
[NEW:L1172]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.applyCount, 2)
[NEW:L1173]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.refreshCount, 2)
[NEW:L1174]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.didRequestFailDueToAuthErrorCount, 2)
[NEW:L1175]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.isRequestAuthenticatedWithCredentialCount, 2)
[NEW:L1176]  +     }

[NEW:L1297]  +

[NEW:L1411]  +     func testThatInterceptorThrowsExcessiveRefreshErrorToAllParallelRequestsAfterExceedingRefreshWindow() {
[NEW:L1412]  +         // Given
[NEW:L1413]  +         // Three parallel requests all get 401, triggering one refresh (a0 → a1). All three retry with a1 and all get
[NEW:L1414]  +         // 401 again. The second refresh attempt is blocked by the refresh window (maximumAttempts: 1 already exhausted).
[NEW:L1415]  +         // All three requests fail with .excessiveRefresh. This exercises the path where isRefreshExcessive fires after
[NEW:L1416]  +         // requestsToRetry has been built up by a prior successful refresh cycle.
[NEW:L1417]  +         let credential = TestCredential()
[NEW:L1418]  +         let authenticator = TestAuthenticator()
[NEW:L1419]  +         let interceptor = AuthenticationInterceptor(authenticator: authenticator,
[NEW:L1420]  +                                                     credential: credential,
[NEW:L1421]  +                                                     refreshWindow: .init(interval: 30, maximumAttempts: 1))
[NEW:L1422]  + 
[NEW:L1423]  +         let requestCount = 3
[NEW:L1424]  +         let session = stored(Session())
[NEW:L1425]  + 
[NEW:L1426]  +         let expect = expectation(description: "all requests should complete")
[NEW:L1427]  +         expect.expectedFulfillmentCount = requestCount
[NEW:L1428]  + 
[NEW:L1429]  +         var requests: [Int: Request] = [:]
[NEW:L1430]  +         var responses: [Int: AFDataResponse<Data?>] = [:]
[NEW:L1431]  + 
[NEW:L1432]  +         // When: Each request hits /status/401 twice: once on the initial attempt (triggering the one allowed refresh),
[NEW:L1433]  +         // and once after retrying with a1 (triggering the excessive check).
[NEW:L1434]  +         for index in 0..<requestCount {
[NEW:L1435]  +             let pathAdapter = PathAdapter(paths: ["/status/401", "/status/401"])
[NEW:L1436]  +             let compositeInterceptor = Interceptor(adapters: [pathAdapter, interceptor], retriers: [interceptor])
[NEW:L1437]  + 
[NEW:L1438]  +             let request = session.request(.default, interceptor: compositeInterceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1439]  +                 responses[index] = $0
[NEW:L1440]  +                 expect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1441]  +             }
[NEW:L1442]  +             requests[index] = request
[NEW:L1443]  +         }
[NEW:L1444]  + 
[NEW:L1445]  +         waitForExpectations(timeout: timeout)
[NEW:L1446]  + 
[NEW:L1447]  +         // Then
[NEW:L1448]  +         for index in 0..<requestCount {
[NEW:L1449]  +             XCTAssertEqual(responses[index]?.result.isFailure, true)
[NEW:L1450]  +             XCTAssertEqual(responses[index]?.result.failure?.asAFError?.isRequestRetryError, true)
[NEW:L1451]  +             XCTAssertEqual(responses[index]?.result.failure?.asAFError?.underlyingError as? AuthenticationError, .excessiveRefresh)
[NEW:L1452]  +             // retryCount is 1, each request was retried once after the first successful refresh, then rejected by the
[NEW:L1453]  +             // excessive check without a second actual retry.
[NEW:L1454]  +             XCTAssertEqual(requests[index]?.retryCount, 1)
[NEW:L1455]  +         }
[NEW:L1456]  + 
[NEW:L1457]  +         // One successful refresh occurred (first batch). The second was blocked.
[NEW:L1458]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.applyCount, 6)
[NEW:L1459]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.refreshCount, 1)
[NEW:L1460]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.didRequestFailDueToAuthErrorCount, 6)
[NEW:L1461]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.isRequestAuthenticatedWithCredentialCount, 6)
[NEW:L1462]  +     }

[NEW:L1464]  +     @MainActor
[NEW:L1465]  +     func testThatInterceptorAllowsProactiveRefreshFromFailedStateUnlikeRetryPath() {
[NEW:L1466]  +         // Given
[NEW:L1467]  +         // After a proactive refresh failure, refreshState is .failed. The retry path has an explicit .failed case that
[NEW:L1468]  +         // returns .doNotRetryWithError immediately. The proactive adapt path has no such gate. It only checks
[NEW:L1469]  +         // isRefreshing (which is false for .failed), so a new request with requiresRefresh: true triggers another
[NEW:L1470]  +         // proactive refresh, overwriting .failed with .refreshing. This test verifies that asymmetry is intentional
[NEW:L1471]  +         // and preserved.
[NEW:L1472]  +         let credential = TestCredential(requiresRefresh: true)
[NEW:L1473]  +         let authenticator = TestAuthenticator(refreshResult: .failure(TestAuthError.refreshNetworkFailure))
[NEW:L1474]  +         let interceptor = AuthenticationInterceptor(authenticator: authenticator, credential: credential)
[NEW:L1475]  +         let session = stored(Session())
[NEW:L1476]  + 
[NEW:L1477]  +         // Phase 1 — proactive refresh fails, putting interceptor into .failed state.
[NEW:L1478]  +         let firstExpect = expectation(description: "first request should complete")
[NEW:L1479]  +         var firstResponse: AFDataResponse<Data?>?
[NEW:L1480]  + 
[NEW:L1481]  +         session.request(.status(200), interceptor: interceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1482]  +             firstResponse = $0
[NEW:L1483]  +             firstExpect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1484]  +         }
[NEW:L1485]  + 
[NEW:L1486]  +         waitForExpectations(timeout: timeout)
[NEW:L1487]  + 
[NEW:L1488]  +         XCTAssertEqual(firstResponse?.result.isFailure, true)
[NEW:L1489]  +         XCTAssertEqual(firstResponse?.result.failure?.asAFError?.isRequestAdaptationError, true)
[NEW:L1490]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.refreshCount, 1)
[NEW:L1491]  + 
[NEW:L1492]  +         // Second, a new request with the same requiresRefresh: true credential adapts. It enters the proactive path,
[NEW:L1493]  +         // appends to adaptOperations, calls refresh(), overwriting .failed with .refreshing. The retry path's .failed
[NEW:L1494]  +         // gate is never reached.
[NEW:L1495]  +         let secondExpect = expectation(description: "second request should complete")
[NEW:L1496]  +         var secondResponse: AFDataResponse<Data?>?
[NEW:L1497]  + 
[NEW:L1498]  +         session.request(.status(200), interceptor: interceptor).validate().response {
[NEW:L1499]  +             secondResponse = $0
[NEW:L1500]  +             secondExpect.fulfill()
[NEW:L1501]  +         }
[NEW:L1502]  + 
[NEW:L1503]  +         waitForExpectations(timeout: timeout)
[NEW:L1504]  + 
[NEW:L1505]  +         // Then: a second refresh was triggered (refreshCount == 2), proving .failed did not block the proactive adapt
[NEW:L1506]  +         // path the way it blocks the retry path.
[NEW:L1507]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.isFailure, true)
[NEW:L1508]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.failure?.asAFError?.isRequestAdaptationError, true)
[NEW:L1509]  +         XCTAssertEqual(secondResponse?.result.failure?.asAFError?.underlyingError as? TestAuthError, .refreshNetworkFailure)
[NEW:L1510]  + 
[NEW:L1511]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.applyCount, 0)
[NEW:L1512]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.refreshCount, 2)
[NEW:L1513]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.didRequestFailDueToAuthErrorCount, 0)
[NEW:L1514]  +         XCTAssertEqual(authenticator.isRequestAuthenticatedWithCredentialCount, 0)
[NEW:L1515]  +     }
[CTX:L1516]    }

[NEW:L27]    + 
[NEW:L28]    + @Suite("MIMEType Initialization")
[NEW:L29]    + struct MIMETypeInitTests {
[NEW:L30]    +     @Test("Parses standard type/subtype pairs", arguments: [("text/plain", "text", "plain"),
[NEW:L31]    +                                                             ("application/json", "application", "json"),
[NEW:L32]    +                                                             ("image/png", "image", "png"),
[NEW:L33]    +                                                             ("*/*", "*", "*"),
[NEW:L34]    +                                                             ("text/*", "text", "*"),
[NEW:L35]    +                                                             ("*/json", "*", "json")])
[NEW:L36]    +     func parsesWellFormedPair(input: String, expectedType: String, expectedSubtype: String) {
[NEW:L37]    +         let mime = Request.MIMEType(input)
[NEW:L38]    +         #expect(mime != nil)
[NEW:L39]    +         #expect(mime?.type == expectedType)
[NEW:L40]    +         #expect(mime?.subtype == expectedSubtype)
[NEW:L41]    +     }
[NEW:L42]    + 
[NEW:L43]    +     @Test("Strips semicolon-delimited parameters from the subtype field")
[NEW:L44]    +     func stripsParameters() {
[NEW:L45]    +         let mime = Request.MIMEType("text/plain; charset=utf-8")
[NEW:L46]    +         #expect(mime?.type == "text")
[NEW:L47]    +         #expect(mime?.subtype == "plain")
[NEW:L48]    +     }
[NEW:L49]    + 
[NEW:L50]    +     @Test("Strips surrounding whitespace before parsing")
[NEW:L51]    +     func stripsWhitespace() {
[NEW:L52]    +         let mime = Request.MIMEType("  text/plain  ")
[NEW:L53]    +         #expect(mime?.type == "text")
[NEW:L54]    +         #expect(mime?.subtype == "plain")
[NEW:L55]    +     }
[NEW:L56]    + 
[NEW:L57]    +     @Test("isWildcard is true only for */*")
[NEW:L58]    +     func wildcardDetection() {
[NEW:L59]    +         #expect(Request.MIMEType("*/*")?.isWildcard == true)
[NEW:L60]    +         #expect(Request.MIMEType("text/*")?.isWildcard == false)
[NEW:L61]    +         #expect(Request.MIMEType("*/json")?.isWildcard == false)
[NEW:L62]    +         #expect(Request.MIMEType("text/plain")?.isWildcard == false)
[NEW:L63]    +     }
[NEW:L64]    + 
[NEW:L65]    +     @Test("Single-component string without '/' must return nil")
[NEW:L66]    +     func singleComponentWithoutSlashReturnsNil() {
[NEW:L67]    +         #expect(Request.MIMEType("text") == nil, "A string with no '/' separator cannot represent a valid MIME type")
[NEW:L68]    +     }
[NEW:L69]    + 
[NEW:L70]    +     @Test("Empty string must return nil")
[NEW:L71]    +     func emptyStringReturnsNil() {
[NEW:L72]    +         #expect(Request.MIMEType("") == nil, "An empty string cannot represent a valid MIME type")
[NEW:L73]    +     }
[NEW:L74]    + }

[NEW:L76]    + // MARK: - MIMEType Matching Tests
[NEW:L77]    + 
[NEW:L78]    + @Suite("MIMEType Matching")
[NEW:L79]    + struct MIMETypeMatchingTests {
[NEW:L80]    +     @Test("Exact type/subtype pair matches itself")
[NEW:L81]    +     func exactMatch() throws {
[NEW:L82]    +         let acceptable = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/plain"))
[NEW:L83]    +         let response = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/plain"))
[NEW:L84]    +         #expect(acceptable.matches(response))
[NEW:L85]    +     }
[NEW:L86]    + 
[NEW:L87]    +     @Test("Wildcard subtype matches any subtype of the same type")
[NEW:L88]    +     func wildcardSubtypeMatchesAnySametype() throws {
[NEW:L89]    +         let textWildcard = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/*"))
[NEW:L90]    +         let textPlain = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/plain"))
[NEW:L91]    +         let textHTML = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/html"))
[NEW:L92]    +         #expect(textWildcard.matches(textPlain))
[NEW:L93]    +         #expect(textWildcard.matches(textHTML))
[NEW:L94]    +     }
[NEW:L95]    + 
[NEW:L96]    +     @Test("Wildcard subtype does not match a different type")
[NEW:L97]    +     func wildcardSubtypeDoesNotMatchDifferentType() throws {
[NEW:L98]    +         let textWildcard = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/*"))
[NEW:L99]    +         let appJSON = try #require(Request.MIMEType("application/json"))
[NEW:L100]   +         #expect(!textWildcard.matches(appJSON))
[NEW:L101]   +     }
[NEW:L102]   + 
[NEW:L103]   +     @Test("Wildcard type matches any type with the same subtype")
[NEW:L104]   +     func wildcardTypeMatchesSameSubtype() throws {
[NEW:L105]   +         let wildcardJSON = try #require(Request.MIMEType("*/json"))
[NEW:L106]   +         let appJSON = try #require(Request.MIMEType("application/json"))
[NEW:L107]   +         #expect(wildcardJSON.matches(appJSON))
[NEW:L108]   +     }
[NEW:L109]   + 
[NEW:L110]   +     @Test("Full wildcard */* matches any MIME type")
[NEW:L111]   +     func fullWildcardMatchesAny() throws {
[NEW:L112]   +         let wildcard = try #require(Request.MIMEType("*/*"))
[NEW:L113]   +         #expect(try wildcard.matches(#require(Request.MIMEType("text/plain"))))
[NEW:L114]   +         #expect(try wildcard.matches(#require(Request.MIMEType("application/json"))))
[NEW:L115]   +         #expect(try wildcard.matches(#require(Request.MIMEType("image/png"))))
[NEW:L116]   +     }
[NEW:L117]   + 
[NEW:L118]   +     @Test("Different type and subtype do not match")
[NEW:L119]   +     func noMatchOnDifferentPair() throws {
[NEW:L120]   +         let appJSON = try #require(Request.MIMEType("application/json"))
[NEW:L121]   +         let textPlain = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/plain"))
[NEW:L122]   +         #expect(!appJSON.matches(textPlain))
[NEW:L123]   +     }
[NEW:L124]   + 
[NEW:L125]   +     @Test("Same type but different subtype does not match without a wildcard")
[NEW:L126]   +     func sameTypeButDifferentSubtypeNoMatch() throws {
[NEW:L127]   +         let textPlain = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/plain"))
[NEW:L128]   +         let textHTML = try #require(Request.MIMEType("text/html"))
[NEW:L129]   +         #expect(!textPlain.matches(textHTML))
[NEW:L130]   +     }
[NEW:L131]   + }
